===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.verifyProvide(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 578-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Verifies that a provide method call has exactly one argument,
   * and that it's a string literal and that the contents of the string are
   * valid JS tokens. Reports a compile error if it doesn't.
   *
   * @return Whether the argument checked out okay
   */
Method code:
578 |   private boolean verifyProvide(NodeTraversal t, Node methodName, Node arg) {
579 |     if (!verifyArgument(t, methodName, arg)) {
580 |       return false;
581 |     }
582 | 
583 |     for (String part : arg.getString().split("\\.")) {
584 |       if (!NodeUtil.isValidPropertyName(part)) {
585 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(arg, INVALID_PROVIDE_ERROR, part));
586 |         return false;
587 |       }
588 |     }
589 |     return true;
590 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 144-229
Documentation:

Method code:
144 |   @Override
145 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
146 |     switch (n.getType()) {
147 |       case Token.CALL:
148 |         boolean isExpr = parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT;
149 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
150 |         if (left.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
151 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
152 |           if (name.getType() == Token.NAME &&
153 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
154 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
155 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
156 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
157 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
158 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
159 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
160 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
161 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
162 |               break;
163 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
164 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
165 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
166 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
167 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
168 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
169 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.STRING) {
170 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
171 |                 if (dot == -1) {
172 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
173 |                 } else {
174 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
175 |                 }
176 |               }
177 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
178 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
179 |               List<String> typeDecls =
180 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
181 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
182 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
183 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
184 |                 }
185 |               }
186 | 
187 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
188 |               // get compiled out.
189 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(0));
190 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
191 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
192 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
193 |             }
194 |           }
195 |         }
196 |         break;
197 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
198 |       case Token.NAME:
199 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
200 |         // object.
201 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
202 |         break;
203 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
204 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
205 |         // error. Hosited functions will explode if the're provided.
206 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
207 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
208 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
209 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
210 |           if (pn != null) {
211 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
212 |           }
213 |         }
214 |         break;
215 | 
216 |       case Token.NEW:
217 |         trySimplifyNewDate(t, n, parent);
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.GETPROP:
221 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME &&
222 |             parent.getType() != Token.CALL &&
223 |             parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
224 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
225 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
226 |         }
227 |         break;
228 |     }
229 |   }

   * Handles a goog.provide call.
   */
Method code:
276 |   private void processProvideCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
277 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
278 |     Node arg = left.getNext();
279 |     if (verifyProvide(t, left, arg)) {
280 |       String ns = arg.getString();
281 |       if (providedNames.containsKey(ns)) {
282 |         ProvidedName previouslyProvided = providedNames.get(ns);
283 |         if (!previouslyProvided.isExplicitlyProvided()) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;
780 |     } else {
781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
785 |       } else {
786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

/**

1538 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {

1543 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";

1547 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
1548 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
1549 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";

1553 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
1554 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
1555 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
1556 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
1557 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
1558 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
1559 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
1560 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
1561 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
1562 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
1563 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
1564 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
1565 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
1566 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
1567 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
1568 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
1569 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
1570 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
1571 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
1572 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 955-975
Documentation:

Method code:
955 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
956 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
957 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
958 |       return s;
959 |     }
960 | 
961 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
962 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
963 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
964 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
965 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
966 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
967 |       // crazy.
968 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
969 |         sb.append(c);
970 |       } else {
971 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
972 |       }
973 |     }
974 |     return sb.toString();
975 |   }

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
399 |   @Override
400 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
401 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
402 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
403 |     if (unknown) {
404 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
405 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
406 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
407 |         unknown = false;

410 |       }
411 |     }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1028-1165
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1028 |   Node parseInputs() {
1029 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1030 | 
1031 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1032 |     // individual file parse trees.
1033 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1034 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1035 |     }
1036 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1037 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1038 |     }
1039 | 
1040 |     // Parse main js sources.
1041 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1042 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1043 | 
1044 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1045 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1046 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1047 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1048 |     }
1049 | 
1050 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1051 | 
1052 |     try {
1053 |       // Parse externs sources.
1054 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1055 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1056 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1057 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1058 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1059 |           return null;
1060 |         }
1061 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1062 |       }
1063 | 
1064 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1065 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1066 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1067 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1068 | 
1069 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1070 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1071 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1072 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1073 |           }
1074 |         }
1075 | 
1076 |         try {
1077 |           inputs =
1078 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1079 |               .manageDependencies(
1080 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1081 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1082 |           report(JSError.make(
1083 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1084 |           return null;
1085 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1086 |           report(JSError.make(
1087 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1088 |           return null;
1089 |         }
1090 |       }
1091 | 
1092 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1093 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1094 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1095 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1096 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1097 |           return null;
1098 |         }
1099 | 
1100 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1101 |         if (n == null) {
1102 |           continue;
1103 |         }
1104 | 
1105 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1106 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1107 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1108 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1109 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1110 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1111 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1112 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1113 | 
1114 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1115 | 
1116 |             externs.add(input);
1117 |             staleInputs = true;
1118 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1119 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1120 |             staleInputs = true;
1121 |           }
1122 |         }
1123 |       }
1124 | 
1125 |       if (staleInputs) {
1126 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1127 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1128 |       }
1129 | 
1130 |       // Build the AST.
1131 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1132 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1133 |         if (n == null) {
1134 |           continue;
1135 |         }
1136 | 
1137 |         if (devMode) {
1138 |           runSanityCheck();
1139 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1140 |             return null;
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1145 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1146 | 
1147 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1148 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1149 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1150 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1151 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1152 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1153 |         }
1154 | 
1155 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1156 |       }
1157 | 
1158 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1159 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1160 | 
1161 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1162 |     } finally {
1163 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1164 |     }
1165 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

   * Handles a candidate definition for a goog.provided name.

300 |   private void handleCandidateProvideDefinition(
301 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
302 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
303 |       String name = null;
304 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME && parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
305 |         name = n.getString();
306 |       } else if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&

313 |           processProvideFromPreviousPass(t, name, parent);

315 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
316 |           if (pn != null) {
317 |             pn.addDefinition(parent, t.getModule());

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 400-416
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
400 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
401 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
402 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
403 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
404 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
405 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
406 |         String inputName = input.getName();
407 | 
408 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
409 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
410 |         // will get an error down the line.
411 |         inputs.add(input);
412 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 |     return inputs;
416 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()

Method code:
425 |     @Override
426 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
427 |       if (lineBreak) {
428 |         if (sawFunction) {
429 |           startNewLine();
430 |           sawFunction = false;
431 |         }
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
435 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
436 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
437 |       int len = code.length();
438 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
439 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
440 |         if (ch == ';') {
441 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |       maybeCutLine();
445 |     }

Method code:
630 |     @Override
631 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
632 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
633 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
634 |       }
635 | 
636 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
637 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
638 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
639 |           reportGetter(el);
640 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
641 |           reportSetter(el);
642 |         } else {
643 |           node.addChildToBack(transformAsString(el.getLeft()));
644 |           node.addChildToBack(transform(el.getRight()));
645 |         }
646 |       }
647 |       return node;
648 |     }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {

78 |     }

80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);

83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }

116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
216 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
220 |       case Token.VOID:
221 |       case Token.NOT:
222 |       case Token.BITNOT:
223 |       case Token.POS:
224 |       case Token.NEG: {
225 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
227 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
228 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
229 |         break;
230 |       }
231 | 
232 |       case Token.HOOK: {
233 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
234 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
235 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
236 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
237 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
239 |         addExpr(last, 1);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.REGEXP:
244 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
245 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
246 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
247 |         }
248 | 
249 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
250 | 
251 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
252 |         if (childCount == 2) {
253 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
254 |         } else {
255 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |           add(regexp);
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 | 
260 |       case Token.GET_REF:
261 |         add(first);
262 |         break;
263 | 
264 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
265 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
266 |         add(first);
267 |         add(".");
268 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
269 |         break;
270 | 
271 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
272 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
273 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
274 |         }
275 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
276 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
277 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
278 |           add("(");
279 |         }
280 | 
281 |         add("function");
282 |         add(first);
283 | 
284 |         add(first.getNext());
285 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
286 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
287 | 
288 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
289 |           add(")");
290 |         }
291 |         break;
292 | 
293 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
294 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
295 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
296 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
297 |         }
298 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
299 |         if (preserveBlock) {
300 |           cc.beginBlock();
301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
304 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
305 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
306 |                 !preserveBlock &&
307 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
308 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
309 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
310 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
311 | 
312 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
313 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
314 |             cc.endStatement();
315 |           }
316 | 
317 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
318 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
319 |           }
320 | 
321 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
322 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
323 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
324 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
325 |           }
326 |         }
327 |         if (preserveBlock) {
328 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
329 |         }
330 |         break;
331 |       }
332 | 
333 |       case Token.FOR:
334 |         if (childCount == 4) {
335 |           add("for(");
336 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
337 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
338 |           } else {
339 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
340 |           }
341 |           add(";");
342 |           add(first.getNext());
343 |           add(";");
344 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
345 |           add(")");
346 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
347 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
348 |         } else {
349 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
350 |           add("for(");
351 |           add(first);
352 |           add("in");
353 |           add(first.getNext());
354 |           add(")");
355 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
356 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
357 |         }
358 |         break;
359 | 
360 |       case Token.DO:
361 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
362 |         add("do");
363 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
364 |         add("while(");
365 |         add(last);
366 |         add(")");
367 |         cc.endStatement();
368 |         break;
369 | 
370 |       case Token.WHILE:
371 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
372 |         add("while(");
373 |         add(first);
374 |         add(")");
375 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
376 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.EMPTY:
380 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
381 |         break;
382 | 
383 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
384 |         Preconditions.checkState(
385 |             childCount == 2,
386 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
387 |         Preconditions.checkState(

391 |         if (needsParens) {
392 |           add("(");

395 |         if (needsParens) {

397 |         }
398 |         add(".");
399 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
400 |         break;
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       case Token.GETELEM:
404 |         Preconditions.checkState(
405 |             childCount == 2,
406 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
407 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
408 |         add("[");
409 |         add(first.getNext());
410 |         add("]");
411 |         break;
412 | 
413 |       case Token.WITH:
414 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
415 |         add("with(");
416 |         add(first);
417 |         add(")");
418 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
419 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
420 |         break;
421 | 
422 |       case Token.INC:
423 |       case Token.DEC: {
424 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
425 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
426 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
427 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
428 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
429 |         if (postProp != 0) {
430 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
431 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
432 |         } else {
433 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
434 |           add(first);
435 |         }
436 |         break;
437 |       }
438 | 
439 |       case Token.CALL:
440 |         // We have two special cases here:
441 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
442 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
443 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
444 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
445 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
446 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
447 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
448 |         // that must be preserved.
449 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
450 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
451 |           add("(0,");
452 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
453 |           add(")");
454 |         } else {
455 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         }
457 |         add("(");
458 |         addList(first.getNext());
459 |         add(")");
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.IF:
463 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
464 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
465 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
466 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
467 |           cc.beginBlock();
468 |         }
469 | 
470 |         add("if(");
471 |         add(first);
472 |         add(")");
473 | 
474 |         if (hasElse) {
475 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
476 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
477 |           add("else");
478 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
479 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
480 |         } else {
481 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
482 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
483 |         }
484 | 
485 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
486 |           cc.endBlock();
487 |         }
488 |         break;
489 | 
490 |       case Token.NULL:
491 |       case Token.THIS:
492 |       case Token.FALSE:
493 |       case Token.TRUE:
494 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
495 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
496 |         break;
497 | 
498 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
499 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
500 |         add("continue");
501 |         if (childCount == 1) {
502 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
503 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
504 |           }
505 |           add(" ");
506 |           add(first);
507 |         }
508 |         cc.endStatement();
509 |         break;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
513 |         add("debugger");
514 |         cc.endStatement();
515 |         break;
516 | 
517 |       case Token.BREAK:
518 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
519 |         add("break");
520 |         if (childCount == 1) {
521 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
522 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
523 |           }
524 |           add(" ");
525 |           add(first);
526 |         }
527 |         cc.endStatement();
528 |         break;
529 | 
530 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
531 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
532 | 
533 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
534 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
535 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
536 |         cc.endStatement();
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.NEW:
540 |         add("new ");
541 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
542 | 
543 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
544 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
545 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
546 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
547 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
548 |         }
549 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
550 | 
551 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
552 |         Node next = first.getNext();
553 |         if (next != null) {
554 |           add("(");
555 |           addList(next);
556 |           add(")");
557 |         }
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.STRING:
561 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
562 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
563 |         break;
564 | 
565 |       case Token.DELPROP:
566 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
567 |         add("delete ");
568 |         add(first);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
573 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
574 |         if (needsParens) {
575 |           add("(");
576 |         }
577 |         add("{");
578 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
579 |           if (c != first) {
580 |             cc.listSeparator();
581 |           }
582 | 
583 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
584 |           // not JavaScript keywords
585 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
586 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
587 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
588 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
589 |               // unicode escaped.
590 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
591 |             add(c.getString());
592 |           } else {
593 |             addExpr(c, 1);
594 |           }
595 |           add(":");
596 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
597 |         }
598 |         add("}");
599 |         if (needsParens) {
600 |           add(")");
601 |         }
602 |         break;
603 |       }
604 | 
605 |       case Token.SWITCH:
606 |         add("switch(");
607 |         add(first);
608 |         add(")");
609 |         cc.beginBlock();
610 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
611 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
612 |         break;
613 | 
614 |       case Token.CASE:
615 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
616 |         add("case ");
617 |         add(first);
618 |         addCaseBody(last);
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add("default");
624 |         addCaseBody(first);
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.LABEL:
628 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
629 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
630 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
631 |         }
632 |         add(first);
633 |         add(":");
634 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
635 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
636 |         break;
637 | 
638 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
639 |       // ignored for our purposes.
640 |       case Token.SETNAME:
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       default:
644 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
648 |   }

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.updateMinimumModule(JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 726-738
Documentation:

Method code:
726 |     private void updateMinimumModule(JSModule newModule) {
727 |       if (minimumModule == null) {
728 |         minimumModule = newModule;
729 |       } else if (moduleGraph != null) {
730 |         minimumModule = moduleGraph.getDeepestCommonDependencyInclusive(
731 |             minimumModule, newModule);
732 |       } else {
733 |         // If there is no module graph, then there must be exactly one
734 |         // module in the program.
735 |         Preconditions.checkState(newModule == minimumModule,
736 |                                  "Missing module graph");
737 |       }
738 |     }

===== M0102 =====

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
241 |     private void annotateConstants(Node n, Node parent) {
242 |       Preconditions.checkState(
243 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME || n.getType() == Token.STRING);
244 | 
245 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
246 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
247 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
248 |       if (n.getType() != Token.STRING
249 |           || parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
250 |         if (NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
251 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
252 |         }
253 |       }
254 |     }

===== M0106 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 83-107
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a module graph from a list of modules in dependency order.
   */
Method code:
83 |   public JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule> modulesInDepOrder) {
84 |     modules = Sets.newHashSetWithExpectedSize(modulesInDepOrder.size());
85 |     modulesByDepth = Lists.newArrayList();
86 | 
87 |     for (JSModule module : modulesInDepOrder) {
88 |       int depth = 0;
89 |       for (JSModule dep : module.getDependencies()) {
90 |         int depDepth = dep.getDepth();
91 |         if (depDepth < 0) {
92 |           throw new ModuleDependenceException(String.format(
93 |               "Modules not in dependency order: %s preceded %s",
94 |               module.getName(), dep.getName()),
95 |               module, dep);
96 |         }
97 |         depth = Math.max(depth, depDepth + 1);
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       module.setDepth(depth);
101 |       modules.add(module);
102 |       if (depth == modulesByDepth.size()) {
103 |         modulesByDepth.add(new ArrayList<JSModule>());
104 |       }
105 |       modulesByDepth.get(depth).add(module);
106 |     }
107 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.registerAnyProvidedPrefixes(String,Node,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 636-651
Documentation:
/**
   * Registers ProvidedNames for prefix namespaces if they haven't
   * already been defined. The prefix namespaces must be registered in
   * order from shortest to longest.
   *
   * @param ns The namespace whose prefixes may need to be provided.
   * @param node The EXPR of the provide call.
   * @param module The current module.
   */
Method code:
636 |   private void registerAnyProvidedPrefixes(
637 |       String ns, Node node, JSModule module) {
638 |     int pos = ns.indexOf('.');
639 |     while (pos != -1) {
640 |       String prefixNs = ns.substring(0, pos);
641 |       pos = ns.indexOf('.', pos + 1);
642 |       if (providedNames.containsKey(prefixNs)) {
643 |         providedNames.get(prefixNs).addProvide(
644 |             node, module, false /* implicit */);
645 |       } else {
646 |         providedNames.put(
647 |             prefixNs,
648 |             new ProvidedName(prefixNs, node, module, false /* implicit */));
649 |       }
650 |     }
651 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
825 |   static int precedence(int type) {
826 |     switch (type) {
827 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
828 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
829 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
830 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
831 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
832 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
833 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
834 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
835 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
836 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
837 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
838 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
839 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
840 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
841 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
842 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
843 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
844 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
845 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
846 |       case Token.EQ:
847 |       case Token.NE:
848 |       case Token.SHEQ:
849 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
850 |       case Token.LT:
851 |       case Token.GT:
852 |       case Token.LE:
853 |       case Token.GE:
854 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
855 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
856 |       case Token.LSH:
857 |       case Token.RSH:
858 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
859 |       case Token.SUB:
860 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
861 |       case Token.MUL:
862 |       case Token.MOD:
863 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
864 |       case Token.INC:
865 |       case Token.DEC:
866 |       case Token.NEW:
867 |       case Token.DELPROP:
868 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
869 |       case Token.VOID:
870 |       case Token.NOT:
871 |       case Token.BITNOT:
872 |       case Token.POS:
873 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
874 | 
875 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
876 |       case Token.CALL:
877 |       case Token.EMPTY:
878 |       case Token.FALSE:
879 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
880 |       case Token.GETELEM:
881 |       case Token.GETPROP:
882 |       case Token.GET_REF:
883 |       case Token.IF:
884 |       case Token.LP:

889 |       case Token.REGEXP:
890 |       case Token.RETURN:
891 |       case Token.STRING:
892 |       case Token.THIS:
893 |       case Token.TRUE:
894 |         return 15;
895 | 
896 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
897 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +

===== M0114 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNode(String,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1702-1722
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a node representing a qualified name.
   *
   * @param name A qualified name (e.g. "foo" or "foo.bar.baz")
   * @param lineno The source line offset.
   * @param charno The source character offset from start of the line.
   * @return A NAME or GETPROP node
   */
Method code:
1702 |   public static Node newQualifiedNameNode(String name, int lineno, int charno) {
1703 |     int endPos = name.indexOf('.');
1704 |     if (endPos == -1) {
1705 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, name, lineno, charno);
1706 |     }
1707 |     Node node = Node.newString(Token.NAME, name.substring(0, endPos),
1708 |                                lineno, charno);
1709 |     int startPos;
1710 |     do {
1711 |       startPos = endPos + 1;
1712 |       endPos = name.indexOf('.', startPos);
1713 |       String part = (endPos == -1
1714 |                      ? name.substring(startPos)
1715 |                      : name.substring(startPos, endPos));
1716 |       node = new Node(Token.GETPROP, node,
1717 |                       Node.newString(Token.STRING, part, lineno, charno),
1718 |                       lineno, charno);
1719 |     } while (endPos != -1);
1720 | 
1721 |     return node;
1722 |   }

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-262
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
241 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
242 |     guards.add(
243 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
244 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
245 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
246 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
247 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
251 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
252 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
253 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
254 |     // error off.
255 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
256 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
257 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
258 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
260 |     }
261 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
262 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
379 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
380 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
381 |          current != null;
382 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
383 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
384 |         return true;
385 |       }
386 |     }
387 |     return false;
388 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-613
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
609 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
610 |     }
611 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
612 |     typeSet.add(owner);
613 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.

1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.makeVarDeclNode(String,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 830-845
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a simple namespace variable declaration
     * (e.g. <code>var foo = {};</code>).
     *
     * @param namespace A simple namespace (must be a valid js identifier)
     * @param sourceNode The node to get source information from.
     */
Method code:
830 |     private Node makeVarDeclNode(String namespace, Node sourceNode) {
831 |       Node name = Node.newString(Token.NAME, namespace);
832 |       name.addChildToFront(createNamespaceLiteral());
833 | 
834 |       Node decl = new Node(Token.VAR, name);
835 |       decl.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
836 | 
837 |       // TODO(nicksantos): ew ew ew. Create a mutator package.
838 |       if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isConstant(namespace)) {
839 |         name.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
840 |       }

===== M0191 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/TokenStream.java
Original range: 294-319
Documentation:

Method code:
294 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
295 |         int length = s.length();
296 | 
297 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
298 |             return false;
299 | 
300 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
301 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
302 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
303 |                 if (c == '\\') {
304 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
305 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
306 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
307 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
308 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
309 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
310 |                         return true;
311 |                      }
312 |                 }
313 | 
314 |                 return false;
315 |             }
316 |         }
317 | 
318 |         return true;
319 |     }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.getDeepestCommonDependency(JSModule,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 163-180
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds the deepest common dependency of two modules, not including the two
   * modules themselves.
   *
   * @param m1 A module in this graph
   * @param m2 A module in this graph
   * @return The deepest common dep of {@code m1} and {@code m2}, or null if
   *     they have no common dependencies
   */
Method code:
163 |   JSModule getDeepestCommonDependency(JSModule m1, JSModule m2) {
164 |     int m1Depth = m1.getDepth();
165 |     int m2Depth = m2.getDepth();
166 |     // According our definition of depth, the result must have a strictly
167 |     // smaller depth than either m1 or m2.
168 |     for (int depth = Math.min(m1Depth, m2Depth) - 1; depth >= 0; depth--) {
169 |       List<JSModule> modulesAtDepth = modulesByDepth.get(depth);
170 |       // Look at the modules at this depth in reverse order, so that we use the
171 |       // original ordering of the modules to break ties (later meaning deeper).
172 |       for (int i = modulesAtDepth.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
173 |         JSModule m = modulesAtDepth.get(i);
174 |         if (dependsOn(m1, m) && dependsOn(m2, m)) {
175 |           return m;
176 |         }
177 |       }
178 |     }
179 |     return null;
180 |   }

/**
   * Finds the deepest common dependency of two modules, including the

   *
   * @param m1 A module in this graph

191 |   public JSModule getDeepestCommonDependencyInclusive(

   * desired type. Reports a compile error if it doesn't.

   */
Method code:
608 |   private boolean verifyArgument(NodeTraversal t, Node methodName, Node arg,
609 |       int desiredType) {
610 |     DiagnosticType diagnostic = null;

612 |       diagnostic = NULL_ARGUMENT_ERROR;

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.

105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

===== M0221 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

1837 |   static boolean isValidPropertyName(String name) {

===== M0251 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()

Original range: 451-467
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
451 |     @Override
452 |     void maybeCutLine() {
453 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
454 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
455 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
456 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
457 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
458 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
459 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
460 |           lineIndex++;
461 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
462 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
463 |         } else {
464 |           startNewLine();
465 |         }
466 |       }
467 |     }

===== M0258 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
183 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
184 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
185 | 
186 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
187 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.

194 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
195 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&

/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1515 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1516 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1517 |       int index = 0;
1518 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1519 |            current != null;

1521 |         if (current == node) {
1522 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1523 |         }
1524 |         index++;
1525 |       }
1526 |     }
1527 |     return false;
1528 |   }

   * Creates a union.

   */

149 |   JSType build() {
150 |     if (result == null) {
151 |       if (isAllType) {

333 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
334 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
335 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;

355 |           sb.append(", ...");

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
820 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
821 |     // unknown
822 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
823 |       return true;
824 |     }
825 |     // equality
826 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
827 |       return true;
828 |     }
829 |     // all type
830 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
831 |       return true;
832 |     }
833 |     // unions
834 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
835 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
836 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
837 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
838 |           return true;
839 |         }
840 |       }
841 |     }
842 |     // named types
843 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
844 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
845 |     }
846 |     return false;
847 |   }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0303 =====
