Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating

67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.isNamespacePlaceholder(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 881-898
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is namespace placeholder.
   */
Method code:
881 |   private static boolean isNamespacePlaceholder(Node n) {
882 |     if (!n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE)) {
883 |       return false;
884 |     }
885 | 
886 |     Node value = null;
887 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
888 |       Node assign = n.getFirstChild();
889 |       value = assign.getLastChild();
890 |     } else if (n.getType() == Token.VAR) {
891 |       Node name = n.getFirstChild();
892 |       value = name.getFirstChild();
893 |     }
894 | 
895 |     return value != null
896 |       && value.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT
897 |       && !value.hasChildren();
898 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-281

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java

Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 565-580
Documentation:

Method code:
565 |     @Override
566 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
567 |       Node n =  newNode(
568 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
569 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
570 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
571 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
572 |       // doesn't do.
573 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
574 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
575 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
576 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
577 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
578 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
579 |       return n;
580 |     }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-197
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     append(newcode);
197 |   }

149 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {

151 |       return true;

383 |     }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

/**
   * Creates an iterable over the transitive dependencies of module {@code m}
   * in a non-increasing depth ordering. The result does not include the module
   * {@code m}.
   *
   * @param m A module in this graph
   * @return The transitive dependencies of module {@code m}
   */
Method code:
221 |   Set<JSModule> getTransitiveDepsDeepestFirst(JSModule m) {
222 |     Set<JSModule> deps = dependencyMap.get(m);
223 |     if (deps != null) {
224 |       return deps;
225 |     }
226 |     deps = new TreeSet<JSModule>(new InverseDepthComparator());
227 |     addDeps(deps, m);
228 |     dependencyMap.put(m, deps);
229 |     return deps;
230 |   }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

Method code:
56 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
57 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
58 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
59 |       addGuards(Iterables.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
60 |     } else {
61 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
62 |       if (index < 0) {
63 |         index = -index - 1;
64 |       }
65 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
66 |     }
67 |   }

===== M0423 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1821-1831
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1821 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1822 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1823 |     int len = s.length();
1824 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1825 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1826 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1827 |         return false;
1828 |       }
1829 |     }
1830 |     return true;
1831 |   }

===== M0443 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 747-809
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
747 |     void replace() {
748 |       if (firstNode == null) {
749 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
750 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
751 |         return;
752 |       }
753 | 
754 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
755 |       // provided symbol.
756 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
757 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
758 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
759 | 
760 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
761 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
762 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(candidateDefinition)) {
763 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
764 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
765 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
766 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
767 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
768 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
769 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
770 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
771 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
772 |             Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
773 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
774 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
775 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
776 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
777 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
778 |             replacementNode = varNode;
779 |           }
780 |         }
781 |       } else {
782 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
783 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
784 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
785 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
786 |         } else {
787 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
788 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
789 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.indexOf('.');
790 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
791 |             // Any old place is fine.
792 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
793 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
794 |           } else {
795 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
796 |             ProvidedName parentName =
797 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
798 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
799 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
800 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
801 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
802 |           }
803 |         }
804 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
805 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
806 |         }
807 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
808 |       }
809 |     }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 294-302
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }
